# Husqvarna FC 450 2025: aftermarket parts

4669 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 432 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 739 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3498 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
